270	## 根因（一句话）
271	
272	`data-theme="future"` 和 `future.css` 都是好的（截图里青色实心按钮就是 future.css 生效的证据），真正的问题是：**dev server 在 future 页面文件创建之前就启动了，Tailwind v4 的自动源文件扫描快照里根本没有 `src/pages/future/` 和 `src/layout/future/` 这两个新目录**，导致这些文件里所有 Tailwind 工具类（`bg-[#0a101c]`、`text-[#eef4fc]` 等）从未被生成，页面只剩下 body 的白色底 + future.css 里手写的那几个 `f-*` 类，于是深色主题整体失效、浅色文字落在白底上看不清。
273	
274	诊断依据：curl 拉取 dev server 转换后的 `index.css`(70KB)，搜索 future 文件独有的颜色值 `0a101c / eef4fc / 22d3ee`,**命中 0 次**；而 POS 文件的同类工具类全部存在。重启 dev server 后重新拉取，`0a101c` 命中 2 次、`eef4fc` 4 次、`22d3ee` 22 次，工具类全部生成。
275	
276	## 改了哪些文件
277	
278	**没有改任何源码文件**——这是构建缓存问题，不是代码问题。唯一的动作：停掉旧 dev server 任务，以 strictPort 重启在 5174（后台任务，正在运行）。`index.css`、`future.css`、所有 future 页面组件经代码审查确认变量链完好（`[data-theme="future"]` 作用域覆盖 shadcn 变量，`@theme inline` 的运行时 `var()` 引用不受层叠顺序影响）。
279

282	`npx tsc -b`:**0 错误**。

287	- **`/f/production`**(f-production.png)：深色侧栏（选中项青色描边+淡青底）、深色标签页（激活态青点+青描边）、单头玻璃卡内 mono 大号单号 `SC20260915003` 和数量 `1,020`、青色实心"打开"按钮+其余玻璃展示态按钮、青色步骤条节点、深色明细表配青色合计数字，所有文字均为 `#dbe4f0 / #eef4fc` 高对比浅色。

713	## 根因（那坨绿色是什么）
714	
715	不是 aurora 漏进弹窗，也不是标题栏样式坏了，而是**弹窗整体尺寸失控把标题栏顶出了屏幕外**，两个叠加的样式冲突：
716	
717	1. shadcn `DialogContent` 默认带 `sm:max-w-lg`(512px)，我传入的 `max-w-3xl` 与它在同一媒体查询下同优先级竞争输了，弹窗实际只有 **512px 宽** → 表头"客户款号"竖排、日期折行。
718	2. 弹窗没有 `max-h` 限制，10 行列表把内容撑到 **1002px 高**，居中定位后 top=-51px → **标题"打开生产通知单"和关闭 X 被顶到视口上方不可见，底部分页被裁到视口下方**。用户看到的"顶部绿色渐变块"就是标题栏消失后、从半透明遮罩透出来的页面 aurora 绿背景。

720	（用 playwright 实测 DOM 确认：title 和 close 按钮在文档里都存在，只是坐标在屏幕外；dialog rect 修复前 512×1002 top=-52，修复后 920×717 top=92。)

856	## 根因

858	不是背景色没生效，而是 **sticky 加错了元素**：我把 `sticky top-0 z-10` 加在 `<thead>` 上，`bg-white` 加在 `<th>` 上。`position: sticky` 在 thead 上能定位（表头确实停住了），但 **table 盒模型内部元素（thead/tr）的 z-index 层叠在浏览器里不可靠**——滚动的 `<tbody>` 行内容绘制在 sticky thead 之上，而行与行之间、行单元格的透明区域让文字透出来，形成"表头镂空、第一行跑到表头上面"的叠影。headless Chromium 下也完整复现了（修复前截图 `sticky-repro.png` 可见 SC20260909002/09001 两行压在表头文字上），所以不是用户浏览器的问题。另外滚动容器有 12px 上 padding，行滚到表头上方的透明缝隙里也会露出一条。

1620	# Writing Plans
1621	
1622	## Overview
1623	
1624	Write comprehensive implementation plans assuming the engineer has zero context for our codebase and questionable taste. Document everything they need to know: which files to touch for each task, code, testing, docs they might need to check, how to test it. Give them the whole plan as bite-sized tasks. DRY. YAGNI. TDD. Frequent commits.
1625	
1626	Assume they are a skilled developer, but know almost nothing about our toolset or problem domain. Assume they don't know good test design very well.
1627	
1628	**Announce at start:** "I'm using the writing-plans skill to create the implementation plan."
1629	
1630	**Context:** If working in an isolated worktree, it should have been created via the `superpowers:using-git-worktrees` skill at execution time.
1631	
1632	**Save plans to:** `docs/superpowers/plans/YYYY-MM-DD-<feature-name>.md`
1633	- (User preferences for plan location override this default)
1634	
1635	## Scope Check
1636	
1637	If the spec covers multiple independent subsystems, it should have been broken into sub-project specs during brainstorming. If it wasn't, suggest breaking this into separate plans — one per subsystem. Each plan should produce working, testable software on its own.
1638	
1639	## File Structure
1640	
1641	Before defining tasks, map out which files will be created or modified and what each one is responsible for. This is where decomposition decisions get locked in.
1642	
1643	- Design units with clear boundaries and well-defined interfaces. Each file should have one clear responsibility.
1644	- You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more reliable when files are focused. Prefer smaller, focused files over large ones that do too much.
1645	- Files that change together should live together. Split by responsibility, not by technical layer.
1646	- In existing codebases, follow established patterns. If the codebase uses large files, don't unilaterally restructure - but if a file you're modifying has grown unwieldy, including a split in the plan is reasonable.
1647	
1648	This structure informs the task decomposition. Each task should produce self-contained changes that make sense independently.
1649	
1650	## Task Right-Sizing
1651	
1652	A task is the smallest unit that carries its own test cycle and is worth a
1653	fresh reviewer's gate. When drawing task boundaries: fold setup,
1654	configuration, scaffolding, and documentation steps into the task whose
1655	deliverable needs them; split only where a reviewer could meaningfully
1656	reject one task while approving its neighbor. Each task ends with an
1657	independently testable deliverable.
1658	
1659	## Bite-Sized Task Granularity
1660	
1661	**Each step is one action (2-5 minutes):**
1662	- "Write the failing test" - step
1663	- "Run it to make sure it fails" - step
1664	- "Implement the minimal code to make the test pass" - step
1665	- "Run the tests and make sure they pass" - step
1666	- "Commit" - step
1667	
1668	## Plan Document Header
1669	
1670	**Every plan MUST start with this header:**
1671	
1672	```markdown
1673	# [Feature Name] Implementation Plan
1674	
1675	> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
1676	
1677	**Goal:** [One sentence describing what this builds]
1678	
1679	**Architecture:** [2-3 sentences about approach]
1680	
1681	**Tech Stack:** [Key technologies/libraries]
1682	
1683	**Spec:** [path to the spec/design doc this plan implements — the plan
1684	argues from the spec, so the spec travels with it; executors read both]
1685	
1686	## Global Constraints
1687	
1688	[The spec's project-wide requirements — version floors, dependency limits,
1689	naming and copy rules, platform requirements — one line each, with exact
1690	values copied verbatim from the spec. Every task's requirements implicitly
1691	include this section.]
1692	
1693	---
1694	```
1695	
1696	## Task Structure
1697	
1698	````markdown
1699	### Task N: [Component Name]
1700	
1701	**Files:**
1702	- Create: `exact/path/to/file.py`
1703	- Modify: `exact/path/to/existing.py:123-145`
1704	- Test: `tests/exact/path/to/test.py`
1705	
1706	**Interfaces:**
1707	- Consumes: [what this task uses from earlier tasks — exact signatures]
1708	- Produces: [what later tasks rely on — exact function names, parameter
1709	  and return types. A task's implementer sees only their own task; this
1710	  block is how they learn the names and types neighboring tasks use.]
1711	
1712	- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**
1713	
1714	```python
1715	def test_specific_behavior():
1716	    result = function(input)
1717	    assert result == expected
1718	```
1719	
1720	- [ ] **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**
1721	
1722	Run: `pytest tests/path/test.py::test_name -v`
1723	Expected: FAIL with "function not defined"
1724	
1725	- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**
1726	
1727	```python
1728	def function(input):
1729	    return expected
1730	```
1731	
1732	- [ ] **Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**
1733	
1734	Run: `pytest tests/path/test.py::test_name -v`
1735	Expected: PASS
1736	
1737	- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**
1738	
1739	```bash
1740	git add tests/path/test.py src/path/file.py
1741	git commit -m "feat: add specific feature"
1742	```
1743	````
1744	
1745	## No Placeholders
1746	
1747	Every step must contain the actual content an engineer needs. These are **plan failures** — never write them:
1748	- "TBD", "TODO", "implement later", "fill in details"
1749	- "Add appropriate error handling" / "add validation" / "handle edge cases"
1750	- "Write tests for the above" (without actual test code)
1751	- "Similar to Task N" (repeat the code — the engineer may be reading tasks out of order)
1752	- Steps that describe what to do without showing how (code blocks required for code steps)
1753	- References to types, functions, or methods not defined in any task
1754	
1755	## Self-Review
1756	
1757	After writing the complete plan, look at the spec with fresh eyes and check the plan against it. This is a checklist you run yourself — not a subagent dispatch.
1758	
1759	**1. Spec coverage:** Skim each section/requirement in the spec. Can you point to a task that implements it? List any gaps.
1760	
1761	**2. Placeholder scan:** Search your plan for red flags — any of the patterns from the "No Placeholders" section above. Fix them.
1762	
1763	**3. Type consistency:** Do the types, method signatures, and property names you used in later tasks match what you defined in earlier tasks? A function called `clearLayers()` in Task 3 but `clearFullLayers()` in Task 7 is a bug.
1764	
1765	If you find issues, fix them inline. No need to re-review — just fix and move on. If you find a spec requirement with no task, add the task.
1766

1845	6	## 背景与动机
1846	7	
1847	8	老前端（web/，React+antd）经多轮迭代后用户反馈三类问题：
1848	9	
1849	10	- **视觉太老**：配色/字体/间距/布局陈旧
1850	11	- **操作不顺**：菜单层级绕、单据说切就切丢数据、常用操作步骤多
1851	12	- **性能不好**：大表格卡顿、切页重复拉数据
1852	13

1857	18	| 决策点 | 结论 |
1858	19	|---|---|

1860	21	| 技术栈 | React 19 + Vite + TypeScript + Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui + TanStack Query/Table + react-hook-form + zod |

2559	# Subagent-Driven Development
2560	
2561	Execute plan by dispatching a fresh implementer subagent per task, a task review (spec compliance + code quality) after each, and a broad whole-branch review at the end.
2562	
2563	**Why subagents:** You delegate tasks to specialized agents with isolated context. By precisely crafting their instructions and context, you ensure they stay focused and succeed at their task. They should never inherit your session's context or history — you construct exactly what they need. This also preserves your own context for coordination work.
2564	
2565	**Core principle:** Fresh subagent per task + task review (spec + quality) + broad final review = high quality, fast iteration
2566	
2567	**Narration:** between tool calls, narrate at most one short line — the
2568	ledger and the tool results carry the record.
2569	
2570	**Continuous execution:** Do not pause to check in with your human partner between tasks. Execute all tasks from the plan without stopping. The only reasons to stop are the four named below, or all tasks complete. "Should I continue?" prompts and progress summaries waste their time — they asked you to execute the plan, so execute it.
2571	
2572	**Rulings, not stalls.** A running plan does not wait on a human. Conflicts,
2573	ambiguities, plan defects, a cap you would have asked to exceed — decide
2574	them. The spec is the binding authority, the plan is its argument, and your
2575	judgment settles what neither answers. Record every decision in the ledger as
2576	`Ruling: <what you decided> — <why> — <what it costs if wrong>`, and keep
2577	going. A wrong ruling costs rework your human partner can see and undo; a
2578	session parked on a question costs their whole day and buys nothing.
2579	
2580	Four things stop you, and only these: an irreversible or destructive
2581	operation; a security-sensitive action; a side effect outside this worktree
2582	that norms say you ask about first (a merge, a push to a shared branch, a
2583	publish); and a plan so broken that every path forward is a guess. For those,
2584	stop and ask.
2585

2599	    "Tasks mostly independent?" -> "Stay in this session?" [label="yes"];

2605	
2606	**vs. Executing Plans (parallel session):**
2607	- Same session (no context switch)
2608	- Fresh subagent per task (no context pollution)
2609	- Review after each task (spec compliance + code quality), broad review at the end
2610	- Faster iteration (no human-in-loop between tasks)

2657	    "Finding conflicts with plan text?" -> "Fix round R of 5: R≤3 resume implementer; R≥4 fresh implementer, more capable model" [label="no"];
2658	    "Fix round R of 5: R≤3 resume implementer; R≥4 fresh implementer, more capable model" -> "Dispatch scoped re-review (./re-review-prompt.md)";
2659	    "Dispatch scoped re-review (./re-review-prompt.md)" -> "All findings addressed?";
2660	    "All findings addressed?" -> "Append completion to ledger, mark todo complete" [label="yes"];
2661	    "All findings addressed?" -> "R = 5?" [label="no"];
2662	    "R = 5?" -> "Fix round R of 5: R≤3 resume implementer; R≥4 fresh implementer, more capable model" [label="no - next round"];
2663	    "R = 5?" -> "Adjudicate each open finding" [label="yes - breaker trips"];
2664	    "Adjudicate each open finding" -> "Any load-bearing finding?";
2665	    "Any load-bearing finding?" -> "Rule and continue; stop only if every path forward is a guess" [label="yes"];
2666	    "Any load-bearing finding?" -> "Park findings in ledger with rulings" [label="no"];
2667	    "Park findings in ledger with rulings" -> "Append completion to ledger, mark todo complete";
2668	    "Append completion to ledger, mark todo complete" -> "More tasks remain?";
2669	    "More tasks remain?" -> "Dispatch implementer subagent (./implementer-prompt.md)" [label="yes"];
2670	    "More tasks remain?" -> "Dispatch final code reviewer (../requesting-code-review/code-reviewer.md)" [label="no"];
2671	    "Dispatch final code reviewer (../requesting-code-review/code-reviewer.md)" -> "Final findings? ONE fix dispatch, one scoped re-review, adjudicate residuals";
2672	    "Final findings? ONE fix dispatch, one scoped re-review, adjudicate residuals" -> "Final review clean: delete this plan's workspace";
2673	    "Final review clean: delete this plan's workspace" -> "Use superpowers:finishing-a-development-branch";
2674	}
2675	```
2676	
2677	## Setup
2678	
2679	Ensure the work happens in an isolated workspace: use
2680	superpowers:using-git-worktrees to create one or verify the existing one.
2681	Never start implementation on a main/master branch without your human
2682	partner's explicit consent.
2683	
2684	Conversation memory does not survive compaction. In real sessions,
2685	controllers that lost their place have re-dispatched entire completed task
2686	sequences — the single most expensive failure observed. Track progress in
2687	a ledger file, not only in todos.
2688	
2689	- Each plan owns a workspace: at skill start, run this skill's
2690	  `scripts/sdd-workspace PLAN_FILE` — it prints the plan's git-ignored
2691	  directory (`<repo-root>/.superpowers/sdd/<plan-basename>/`), home to
2692	  every artifact for THIS plan: ledger, briefs, reports, review packages.
2693	  Another plan's directory is never yours to read or write.
2694	- Check for this plan's ledger at `<workspace>/progress.md`. If its first
2695	  line names your plan file, tasks with a `Task <N>: complete` line are DONE
2696	  — do not re-dispatch them; resume at the first task without one. A task
2697	  whose last line is a fix round is mid-loop: resume the loop at the next
2698	  round. A ledger whose first line names a different plan file — or a stray
2699	  ledger at the old flat path `.superpowers/sdd/progress.md` — is another
2700	  plan's progress: leave it in place and start your own, fresh.
2701	- Create the ledger with its identity as the first line:
2702	  `# SDD ledger — plan: <plan file path>`.
2703	- The ledger is your recovery map: the commits it names exist in git even
2704	  when your context no longer remembers creating them. After compaction,
2705	  trust the ledger and `git log` over your own recollection.
2706	- `git clean -fdx` will destroy the workspace (it's git-ignored scratch); if
2707	  that happens, recover from `git log`.
2708	
2709	Read the plan once, note its context and Global Constraints, and create a
2710	todo per task. If the plan names a Spec, read that too: the spec is the
2711	authority the plan argues from, and conflicts inside the plan resolve
2712	against it. A plan with no reachable spec gets a ledger note saying so —
2713	rulings made without one are provisional.
2714	
2715	Before dispatching Task 1, scan the plan once for conflicts, writing down
2716	what you checked as you check it:
2717	
2718	- tasks that contradict each other or the plan's Global Constraints
2719	- anything the plan explicitly mandates that the review rubric treats as a
2720	  defect (a test that asserts nothing, verbatim duplication of a logic block)
2721	
2722	The scan's output is a table, not a verdict. One row for every pair of tasks
2723	that share a file or an interface: the two tasks, what one produces against
2724	what the other consumes, and what you found. One row for every task: whether
2725	its own text agrees with itself — the tests it specifies against the code it
2726	specifies, the files it creates against the files it later touches. "The scan
2727	is clean" without those rows is not a scan you ran.
2728	
2729	Write the table to the ledger. Rule on everything you find before execution
2730	begins — each finding against the plan text that mandates it — and record
2731	each ruling in the ledger. If the scan is clean, proceed without comment.
2732	Rule on each conflict it surfaces — the spec is the binding authority, the
2733	plan is its argument — record the ruling beside its row, and dispatch
2734	Task 1. The review loop remains the net for conflicts that only emerge from
2735	implementation.
2736	
2737	## Model Selection
2738	
2739	Use the least powerful model that can handle each role to conserve cost and increase speed.
2740	
2741	**Mechanical implementation tasks** (isolated functions, clear specs, 1-2 files): use a fast, cheap model. Most implementation tasks are mechanical when the plan is well-specified.
2742	
2743	**Integration and judgment tasks** (multi-file coordination, pattern matching, debugging): use a standard model.
2744	
2745	**Architecture and design tasks**: use the most capable available model.
2746	The final whole-branch review is one of these — dispatch it on the most
2747	capable available model, not the session default.
2748	
2749	**Review tasks**: choose the model with the same judgment, scaled to the
2750	diff's size, complexity, and risk. A small mechanical diff does not need the
2751	most capable model; a subtle concurrency change does. Scoped re-reviews of
2752	small fix diffs take a cheap-to-mid tier.
2753	
2754	**Fix-loop escalation (rounds 4-5)**: use a model at least one tier above
2755	the implementer that got stuck.
2756	
2757	**Always specify the model explicitly when dispatching a subagent.** An
2758	omitted model inherits your session's model — often the most capable and
2759	most expensive — which silently defeats this section.
2760	
2761	**Turn count beats token price.** Wall-clock and context cost scale with how
2762	many turns a subagent takes, and the cheapest models routinely take 2-3× the
2763	turns on multi-step work — costing more overall. Use a mid-tier model as the
2764	floor for reviewers and for implementers working from prose descriptions.
2765	When the task's plan text contains the complete code to write, the
2766	implementation is transcription plus testing: use the cheapest tier for
2767	that implementer. Single-file mechanical fixes also take the cheapest tier.
2768	
2769	**Task complexity signals (implementation tasks):**
2770	- Touches 1-2 files with a complete spec → cheap model
2771	- Touches multiple files with integration concerns → standard model
2772	- Requires design judgment or broad codebase understanding → most capable model
2773	
2774	## The Task Loop
2775	
2776	**Batch small same-shape work.** When the plan lists several tasks that are
2777	each a small, independent edit of the same kind — the same one-line fix,
2778	constant change, or field addition repeated across files — do not dispatch
2779	one subagent per task. Compose ONE dispatch brief listing every file and
2780	its change, send the whole batch to a single subagent, and review its diff
2781	as one unit. Reserve one-dispatch-per-task for work that needs its own
2782	judgment, its own tests, or its own review surface.
2783	
2784	Everything you paste into a dispatch prompt — and everything a subagent
2785	prints back — stays resident in your context for the rest of the session
2786	and is re-read on every later turn. Hand artifacts over as files.
2787	
2788	**Waiting on dispatched subagents:** never poll a wait interface with
2789	short timeouts, and never sit in one silent, open-ended wait either.
2790	While you have local work — ledger updates, packaging the next review,
2791	reading reports — keep working; child results arrive on their own.
2792	When you are genuinely idle, wait in bounded stretches (five to ten
2793	minutes, where your platform allows), and between stretches post one
2794	line of status and reconcile your live children: list them, and chase
2795	any that finished without reporting. A bounded stretch keeps nearly
2796	all of a long wait's efficiency while guaranteeing a stuck or lost
2797	child is noticed within minutes, not at the end of the session.
2798	
2799	### 1. Dispatch the implementer
2800	
2801	Record BASE (`git rev-parse HEAD`) before dispatching — the review package
2802	and fix-round diffs need it.
2803	
2804	- **Task brief:** before dispatching an implementer, run this skill's
2805	  `scripts/task-brief PLAN_FILE N` — it extracts the task's full text to a
2806	  uniquely named file and prints the path. Compose the dispatch so the
2807	  brief stays the single source of
2808	  requirements. Your dispatch should contain: (1) one line on where this
2809	  task fits in the project; (2) the brief path, introduced as "read this
2810	  first — it is your requirements, with the exact values to use verbatim";
2811	  (3) interfaces and decisions from earlier tasks that the brief cannot
2812	  know; (4) your resolution of any ambiguity you noticed in the brief;
2813	  (5) the report-file path and report contract. Exact values (numbers,
2814	  magic strings, signatures, test cases) appear only in the brief. Never
2815	  make a subagent read the whole plan file.
2816	- **Report file:** name the implementer's report file after the brief
2817	  (brief `…/task-N-brief.md` → report `…/task-N-report.md`) and put it in
2818	  the dispatch prompt. The implementer writes the full report there and
2819	  returns only status, commits, a one-line test summary, and concerns.
2820	- A dispatch prompt describes one task, not the session's history. Do not
2821	  paste accumulated prior-task summaries ("state after Tasks 1-3") into
2822	  later dispatches — a real session's dispatch hit 42k chars of which 99%
2823	  was pasted history. A fresh subagent needs its task, the interfaces it
2824	  touches, and the global constraints. Nothing else.
2825	- The dispatch carries the no-subagents contract (it is in the
2826	  implementer template): the implementer never dispatches subagents —
2827	  not helpers, and never a reviewer. Review arrives from you, after the
2828	  report. In real sessions, every reviewer a worker spawned duplicated
2829	  the task review the controller dispatched anyway — a full extra
2830	  review seat per task.
2831	- If an earlier task parked a finding in the area this task touches, carry
2832	  a pointer to that ledger entry in the dispatch.
2833	- Record the implementer's agent identity from the dispatch result —
2834	  fix-loop rounds 1-3 resume this agent.
2835	- Never dispatch multiple implementation subagents in parallel (conflicts).
2836	
2837	Template: [implementer-prompt.md](implementer-prompt.md)
2838	
2839	### 2. Handle the report
2840	
2841	Implementer subagents report one of four statuses. Handle each appropriately:
2842	
2843	**DONE:** Generate the review package (`scripts/review-package PLAN_FILE BASE HEAD`, from this skill's directory — it prints the unique file path it wrote; BASE is the commit you recorded before dispatching the implementer — never `HEAD~1`, which silently drops all but the last commit of a multi-commit task), then dispatch the task reviewer with the printed path.
2844	
2845	**DONE_WITH_CONCERNS:** The implementer completed the work but flagged doubts. Read the concerns before proceeding. If the concerns are about correctness or scope, address them before review. If they're observations (e.g., "this file is getting large"), note them and proceed to review.
2846	
2847	**NEEDS_CONTEXT:** The implementer needs information that wasn't provided. Provide the missing context and re-dispatch.
2848	
2849	**BLOCKED:** The implementer cannot complete the task. Assess the blocker:
2850	1. If it's a context problem, provide more context and re-dispatch with the same model
2851	2. If the task requires more reasoning, re-dispatch with a more capable model
2852	3. If the task is too large, break it into smaller pieces
2853	4. If the plan itself is wrong, rule on the correction, ledger it, and re-dispatch with the ruling carried in the dispatch
2854	
2855	**Never** ignore an escalation or force the same model to retry without changes. If the implementer said it's stuck, something needs to change.
2856	
2857	If the implementer asks questions — before starting or mid-task — answer
2858	clearly and completely, provide additional context if needed, and don't
2859	rush it into implementation.
2860	
2861	### 3. Review the task
2862	
2863	Per-task reviews are task-scoped gates. The broad review happens once, at the
2864	final whole-branch review. Never skip the task review, and never accept a
2865	report missing either verdict — spec compliance AND task quality are both
2866	required. Implementer self-review never replaces the task review; both are
2867	needed.
2868	
2869	- Hand the reviewer its diff as a file: run this skill's
2870	  `scripts/review-package PLAN_FILE BASE HEAD` and pass the reviewer the file path
2871	  it prints (or, without bash: `git log --oneline`, `git diff --stat`,
2872	  and `git diff -U10` for the range, redirected to one uniquely named
2873	  file). The output never enters your own context, and the reviewer sees
2874	  the commit list, stat summary, and full diff with context in one Read
2875	  call. Use the BASE you recorded before dispatching the implementer —
2876	  never `HEAD~1`, which silently truncates multi-commit tasks. Never
2877	  dispatch a task reviewer without a diff file.
2878	- **Reviewer inputs:** the task reviewer gets three paths — the same brief
2879	  file, the report file, and the review package — plus the global
2880	  constraints that bind the task.
2881	- The global-constraints block you hand the reviewer is its attention
2882	  lens. Copy the binding requirements verbatim from the plan's Global
2883	  Constraints section or the spec: exact values, exact formats, and the
2884	  stated relationships between components ("same layout as X", "matches
2885	  Y"). The reviewer's template already carries the process rules (YAGNI,
2886	  test hygiene, review method) — the constraints block is for what THIS
2887	  project's spec demands.
2888	- Do not add open-ended directives like "check all uses" or "run race tests
2889	  if useful" without a concrete, task-specific reason
2890	- Do not ask a reviewer to re-run tests the implementer already ran on the
2891	  same code — the implementer's report carries the test evidence
2892	- Do not pre-judge findings for the reviewer — never instruct a reviewer to
2893	  ignore or not flag a specific issue. If you believe a finding would be a
2894	  false positive, let the reviewer raise it and adjudicate it in the review
2895	  loop. If the prompt you are writing contains "do not flag," "don't treat X
2896	  as a defect," "at most Minor," or "the plan chose" — stop: you are
2897	  pre-judging, usually to spare yourself a review loop.
2898	The task reviewer may report "⚠️ Cannot verify from diff" items — requirements
2899	that live in unchanged code or span tasks. These do not block the rest of the
2900	review, but you must resolve each one yourself before marking the task
2901	complete: you hold the plan and cross-task context the reviewer
2902	lacks. If you confirm an item is a real gap, treat it as a failed spec
2903	review — it enters the fix loop with the other findings.
2904	
2905	Template: [task-reviewer-prompt.md](task-reviewer-prompt.md)
2906	
2907	### 4. The fix loop
2908	
2909	The loop triggers when the review reports spec ❌, any Critical or Important
2910	finding, or a ⚠️ item you confirmed as a real gap.
2911	
2912	Before the loop starts, two routes leave it immediately:
2913	
2914	- Record Minor findings in the progress ledger as you go
2915	  (`Task <N>: minor (deferred): <one-liner>`), and point the final
2916	  whole-branch review at that list so it can triage which must be fixed
2917	  before merge. A roll-up nobody reads is a silent discard. Minor findings
2918	  never enter the loop.
2919	- A finding labeled plan-mandated — or any finding that conflicts with
2920	  what the plan's text requires — is yours to rule on: weigh the finding
2921	  against the plan text, decide with the spec as the binding authority, and
2922	  ledger the ruling before you act on it. Do not dismiss the finding because
2923	  the plan mandates it, and do not dispatch a fix that contradicts the plan
2924	  without a recorded ruling.
2925	Everything else enters the loop. A fix round is one fix dispatch plus one
2926	scoped re-review. Five rounds maximum per task:
2927	
2928	**Rounds 1-3 — resume the original implementer.** Send it the open findings
2929	verbatim. Its context is intact: it knows the task, the code, and its own
2930	choices. If your harness cannot send another message to a live subagent,
2931	dispatch a fresh implementer carrying the brief path, the report-file path,
2932	and the findings — the report file is the persistent memory either way.
2933	
2934	**Rounds 4-5 — dispatch a fresh implementer on a more capable model** (per
2935	Model Selection), with the brief path, the report-file path, the open
2936	findings, and this framing: "A prior implementer attempted this task
2937	[N] times; you own it now. Read the report file for what was tried." A loop
2938	that survives three resumes usually means the implementer cannot see its
2939	own problem — fresh eyes and a capability bump in one move.
2940	
2941	**Every round, either way:** the implementer fixes, re-runs the tests
2942	covering the amended code, appends its fix report to the same report file,
2943	and returns the short contract. Before re-dispatching the reviewer, confirm
2944	the fix report contains the covering tests, the command run, and the
2945	output; dispatch the re-review once all three are present. Name the
2946	covering test files in the fix message — a one-line fix does not need the
2947	whole suite.
2948	
2949	**The re-review is scoped.** Run `scripts/review-package PLAN_FILE FIX_BASE HEAD`
2950	where FIX_BASE is the head the previous review saw, and dispatch
2951	[re-review-prompt.md](re-review-prompt.md) with the findings list, the
2952	brief, the report file, and the printed diff path. The re-reviewer verdicts
2953	each finding ADDRESSED or NOT ADDRESSED and flags new breakage in the fix
2954	diff only. New Critical/Important breakage in the fix diff joins the open
2955	findings list. Out-of-scope observations go to the ledger as deferred
2956	minors — they never extend the loop.
2957	
2958	**After each round,** append to the ledger:
2959	`Task <N>: fix round <R>/5 (<X> addressed, <Y> open — <finding one-liners>; commits <a7>..<b7>)`
2960	
2961	Never fix findings yourself in the controller session — your context stays
2962	clean for coordination, and controller fixes skip review.
2963	
2964	**The breaker.** When round 5's re-review still leaves findings open, stop
2965	dispatching. Adjudicate each open finding yourself — you hold the plan and
2966	the cross-task context the reviewer lacks:
2967	
2968	- **The reviewer is wrong, or the point is contestable:** park it —
2969	  `Task <N>: parked — <finding> — Ruling: <why the code stands>`. The final
2970	  review sees both sides.
2971	- **Real, but nothing downstream builds on it:** park it the same way, with
2972	  a ruling that says it's real and deferred.
2973	- **Real and load-bearing** — a later task builds on it, or it reveals a
2974	  plan defect: rule on the smallest change that unblocks the dependent work,
2975	  ledger it as `Task <N>: Ruling: <finding> — <what you decided and why>`,
2976	  and carry it into the next task's dispatch. Parking a structural failure
2977	  silently lets every dependent task build on it. Stop only when the defect
2978	  leaves every path forward a guess.
2979	
2980	Adjudicate only at the cap. Adjudicating earlier to end a loop is
2981	pre-judging with a different name. Every adjudication is a ledger entry —
2982	a silent discard is forbidden.
2983	
2984	### 5. Complete the task
2985	
2986	When the review comes back clean — or every open finding is parked with a
2987	ruling at the cap — append the completion line to the ledger in the same
2988	message as your other bookkeeping:
2989	
2990	- `Task <N>: complete (commits <base7>..<head7>, review clean)`
2991	- `Task <N>: complete (commits <base7>..<head7>, <K> parked)` after a
2992	  tripped breaker
2993	
2994	Then mark the todo complete and move on. Never move to the next task while
2995	the review has open Critical/Important issues that are neither fixed nor
2996	parked-with-ruling at the cap.
2997	
2998	## Final Review
2999	
3000	The final whole-branch review gets a package too: run
3001	`scripts/review-package PLAN_FILE MERGE_BASE HEAD` (MERGE_BASE = the commit the
3002	branch started from, e.g. `git merge-base main HEAD`) and include the
3003	printed path in the final review dispatch, so the final reviewer reads
3004	one file instead of re-deriving the branch diff with git commands. Dispatch
3005	on the most capable available model (see Model Selection), using
3006	superpowers:requesting-code-review's
3007	[code-reviewer.md](../requesting-code-review/code-reviewer.md). Point it at
3008	the ledger's deferred-minor and parked lines so it can triage which must be
3009	fixed before merge.
3010	
3011	If the final whole-branch review returns findings, dispatch ONE fix subagent
3012	with the complete findings list — not one fixer per finding.
3013	Per-finding fixers each rebuild context and re-run suites; a real
3014	session's final-review fix wave cost more than all its tasks combined.
3015	Then run exactly one scoped re-review of the fix wave
3016	(`scripts/review-package PLAN_FILE FIX_BASE HEAD` over the fix range,
3017	[re-review-prompt.md](re-review-prompt.md)).
3018	Adjudicate any residual findings as in the task loop's breaker: park with
3019	rulings, or rule on the load-bearing ones and ledger what you decided. Only
3020	the four classes above stop you here. There is no second fix wave —
3021	residual load-bearing findings surface to your human partner when
3022	finishing-a-development-branch presents the options.
3023	
3024	## Finish
3025	
3026	Before you delete anything, collect every ledger line containing `Ruling:` —
3027	preflight rulings, parked findings, breaker adjudications, all of them — into
3028	your final message under "Rulings I made", in the order you made them, each
3029	with what it costs if wrong. The list is exhaustive: if the ledger holds a
3030	ruling, the list holds it. That list is the only place the decisions you
3031	took on your human partner's behalf reach them — they read it and rework
3032	whatever you got wrong. A ruling that dies with the workspace was a decision
3033	made in secret.
3034	
3035	When the final whole-branch review is clean and its fixes are merged,
3036	delete this plan's workspace (`rm -rf <workspace>`) — the git history is
3037	the record now. Sibling directories belong to other plans; leave them
3038	alone.
3039	
3040	Use superpowers:finishing-a-development-branch.
3041	
3042	## Common Rationalizations
3043	
3044	| Excuse | Reality |
3045	|--------|---------|
3046	| "Close enough on spec compliance" | Reviewer found spec gaps = not done. Fix or hit the cap and adjudicate — those are the only exits. |
3047	| "I'll fix it myself, dispatching is overhead" | Controller fixes pollute your context and skip review. Resume the implementer. |
3048	| "One more round will converge" | Past the cap, rounds don't converge — the failure is structural. Adjudicate and route. |
3049	| "The reviewer will just find something new anyway" | Scoped re-reviews verify fixes; they cannot wander. New findings on untouched code go to the ledger, not the loop. |
3050	| "This finding is obviously wrong, I'll drop it" | You adjudicate only at the cap, and every ruling is a ledger entry. Silent discards are forbidden. |
3051	| "The fix was small, skip the re-review" | Unreviewed fixes are how regressions land. Every round ends with a scoped re-review. |
3052	| "Reviews slow the loop down" | The loop without reviews is just unverified churn. Reviews are the loop's brakes and steering. |
3053	| "Ledger bookkeeping is overhead" | The ledger is what survives compaction. Controllers without one have re-dispatched entire completed task sequences. |
3054	| "The implementer spawned its own reviewer — free extra assurance" | It's a duplicate seat reviewing the same diff; the task review is the gate. A worker-spawned reviewer is a defect to flag, not rigor. |
3055	
3056	## Example Workflow
3057	
3058	```
3059	You: I'm using Subagent-Driven Development to execute this plan.
3060	
3061	[Setup: worktree verified]
3062	[Read plan file once: docs/superpowers/plans/feature-plan.md]
3063	[Resolve workspace: scripts/sdd-workspace docs/superpowers/plans/feature-plan.md — no ledger inside, fresh start]
3064	[Create todos for all tasks]
3065	
3066	Task 1: Hook installation script
3067	
3068	[Run task-brief for Task 1; dispatch implementer with brief + report paths + context]
3069	
3070	Implementer: "Before I begin - should the hook be installed at user or system level?"
3071	
3072	You: "User level (~/.config/superpowers/hooks/)"
3073	
3074	Implementer: [Later]
3075	  - Implemented install-hook command
3076	  - Added tests, 5/5 passing
3077	  - Self-review: Found I missed --force flag, added it
3078	  - Committed
3079	
3080	[Run review-package PLAN_FILE BASE HEAD; dispatch task reviewer with the printed path]
3081	Task reviewer: Spec ✅ - all requirements met, nothing extra.
3082	  Strengths: Good test coverage, clean. Issues: None. Task quality: Approved.
3083	
3084	[Ledger: Task 1: complete (commits a1b2c3d..d4e5f6a, review clean)]
3085	
3086	Task 2: Recovery modes
3087	
3088	[Run task-brief for Task 2; dispatch implementer with brief + report paths + context]
3089	
3090	Implementer: [No questions]
3091	  - Added verify/repair modes
3092	  - 8/8 tests passing
3093	  - Committed
3094	
3095	[Run review-package PLAN_FILE BASE HEAD; dispatch task reviewer with the printed path]
3096	Task reviewer: Spec ❌:
3097	  - Missing: Progress reporting (spec says "report every 100 items")
3098	  Issues (Important): Magic number (100)
3099

4076	## 发现 1(Important,useDocTabs.closeTab 误导航）—— NOT ADDRESSED
4077	
4078	**diff 内的修复本身是正确的**（针对被引用行）:
4079	
4080	- `web2/src/hooks/useDocTabs.ts:27-29` — `close(path)` 前先记录 `wasActive = active === path`，仅 `wasActive` 时才 `navigate(next ?? "/")`，逻辑与要求一致；`TabsContext.close` 确实只对关闭激活标签返回相邻 key、后台标签返回 `null`(`web2/src/layout/TabsContext.tsx:41-44`)，与注释吻合。
4081	- 两个新测试（`web2/src/__tests__/docTabs.test.tsx:36-60`）是真实断言且覆盖正反两路：关激活 `/b` → 断言 `loc` 变为 `/a`；关后台 `/a` → 断言 `loc` 停留 `/b`。用 userEvent 实际点击、断言路由与标签列表，非空断言。
4082	
4083	**但该发现的用户可见缺陷并未消除**：`useDocTabs` 在生产代码中**没有任何调用方**（全仓 grep 仅命中 hook 自身和新测试）。真实 UI 的关闭按钮走的是 `MainLayout` 里 `TabBar` 自己的路径：
4084	
4085	- `web2/src/layout/MainLayout.tsx:93` — 关闭按钮 `onClosed(close(t.key))`
4086	- `web2/src/layout/MainLayout.tsx:149` — `onClosed={(next) => navigate(next ?? "/")}`
4087	
4088	关闭后台标签时 `close()` 返回 `null`，于是 `navigate(null ?? "/")` → 照样把用户拽回首页（`showHome` 判定后渲染宫格首页，MainLayout.tsx:130,152）。即发现描述的"关非激活标签被拽回首页"在真实操作路径上原样复现。修复加在了一个死代码 hook 上，验收要求"仅关闭激活标签时才导航"作为应用行为未达成。修复方向应该是：让 `TabBar` 的 `onClosed` 只在关闭激活标签时导航（或在 `close` 返回处区分），或直接复用修好后的 `closeTab`。
4089

4091	
4092	- `web2/src/__tests__/docSkeleton.test.tsx` 新增用例（diff 行 64-82，落盘文件同位置）:stub 权限接口只返回「系统用户」行，在 `/production` 渲染 `DocToolbar`(`menuKeyForPath("/production")` → "生产通知单",`web2/src/nav/menu.ts:39`),`waitFor` 断言 `queryByText("打开")` 不在文档中。真实走通 `usePerms.ts:25` 的"菜单行缺失 → false"分支和 `DocToolbar.tsx:75` 的过滤，加载完成后按钮从"乐观放行"消失也有 `waitFor` 兜底，断言真实。

4300	## 1. (Important) MainLayout 真实关闭路径误导航 —— ADDRESSED
4301	
4302	- **两份关闭逻辑已消除**：全仓 grep `navigate(next` 仅命中 `web2/src/hooks/useDocTabs.ts:30` 一处（`if (wasActive) navigate(next ?? "/")`)，无第二份实现。
4303	- **MainLayout 不再有自己的 onClosed navigate**:Shell 侧已是裸 `<TabBar />`(`web2/src/layout/MainLayout.tsx:143`)，旧的 `onClosed={(next) => navigate(next ?? "/")}` 已删除；Shell 剩余的 navigate 用途是 TopBar/HomePage 导航与部门切换（`MainLayout.tsx:113-122,137-149`)，与标签关闭无关。
4304	- **TabBar 关闭按钮走 useDocTabs.closeTab**：关闭按钮 onClick 为 `closeTab(t.key)`(`MainLayout.tsx:85-88`)，激活点击走 `openTab(t.key, t.title)`(`MainLayout.tsx:77`);`TabBar` 无 props，从 `useDocTabs()` 取 `tabs/openTab/closeTab`(`MainLayout.tsx:56-58`)。

4522	## Spec 合规（逐条，对照需求书 Step 1)

4533	| 明细行编辑/比例/分析 | ✅ | 回填、色码行增删改、数量列回合计、比例可改均断言 |
4534	| 展开更多字段 | ✅ | 已审核只读卡内断言（审核人=经理） |

4539	## 重点核查结果
4540	
4541	- **BOM-PO 绑定**：测试真实断言文案全串 `已绑定 PO:PO-OTHER、PO-OTHER2,是否继续使用?`、两个 PO 号列出、**未确认前 BOM款号 为空**、点「继续使用」后采用（双绑由后端保存时幂等绑定，与老系统 `web/src/api/styles.ts:147` 注释口径一致，前端只查询，行为对齐）。对照老 `poBinding.test.tsx` 的 6 个场景：**缺「绑定查询失败：直接放行不弹框」**（老文件 125 行有，新 4 个 it 未含；实现 `ProductionPage.tsx:983-984` 有 catch 放行分支但无测试覆盖，报告清单 #15 声称覆盖「查询失败」不属实）。
4542	- **测试质量抽查**（新建/保存POST/删除/反审核含BOM/BOM-PO/脱敏共 6+ 条）：全部为行为与载荷断言，无快照堆砌。

4544	- **menuKey="生产制单"**：正确。已核实后端 `MenuCatalog.cs:15`、`ProductionController.cs:17`、老系统 `MENU` 常量均为「生产制单」，显式传参解决了菜单树 label「生产通知单」与权限菜单名不一致的问题。DocToolbar 的 `menuKey` 是既有 prop，骨架只消费未改签名。

5095	- 退仓单 = 退回供应商（审核即扣库存）✅ — 测试断言 POST `/api/purchase-returns/{单号}/approve`（diff:615-629）；后端实证 `MaterialInventoryService.cs:27` 审核退仓扣库存；表头含入仓单号、无送货单号、电脑单号后端生成（diff:566-579）

5349	## 重点核查结果
5350	
5351	- **三级链测试真实性**：✅ `plasticIssue.test.tsx`「审核流转」两例真实断言精确端点（`/supervisor-approve`、`/manager-approve`、`/approve`、`/unapprove` 全 URL 匹配）+ 按钮逐级出现/消失 + 「已审核」状态翻转 + 删除按钮消现，非空转。
5352	- **28 个新测试抽查**：实际通读全部 28 例（2 打开 + 4 批量领料 + 2 手选校验 + 2 审核 + 2 查询 + 1 打印 + 4 权限 + 11 纯函数），抽查的 5+ 例（多单调入的 `档=塑胶&按货号=true` 双参数断言、确定带入的空白行替换与数量 10/7 顺序、三级链、保存三段拦截不发 POST、权限位 4 例）均为实质断言。
5353	- **测试运行状态**：按审查方法未重跑；「113 全过 / tsc 0 错 / oxlint 无新增 / 老 web 424 全过」为报告自报，未独立验证。
5354	
5355	## 发现
5356	
5357	**Critical**：无。
5358	**Important**：无。
5359	
5360	**Minor**：
5361	1. `web2/src/pages/PlasticIssuePage.tsx:439` LinesView 查看态比老查看态多出 规格/仓位号/备注 三列（老=同 11 列禁用输入框）。信息超集、无害，但属保真偏差，报告未自报。
5362	2. `web2/src/pages/PlasticIssuePage.tsx:1324`、`:1386` 物料/生产制单选择弹窗无加载态（basis 弹窗有骨架，这两个只有「无匹配」空态），加载期间表格空白。
5363	3. `web2/src/pages/PlasticIssuePage.tsx:431` 数量合计 `sumQty(rows)` 无千分位（老 antd Statistic 默认分组显示），大数可读性略差。
5364	4. `web2/src/pages/PlasticIssuePage.tsx:548` `ColumnDef<PlasticIssueHeader, any>[]` 用了 `any`（oxlint 未报错，与项目现状兼容，仅记录）。
5365	
5366	## ⚠️ 项（报告自报的 deferred 判定）
5367	
5368	- **批量审核未移植**（老 `PlasticIssueFormPage.tsx:101-116`、`:270`，依附内嵌历史列表 rowSelection）：**可 deferred**。OpenDocDialog 取代内嵌列表是 Task 5 起已确立的架构口径，批量审核失去载体属架构替代的连带结果；但这是相对老系统的真实功能缺口，建议登记跟进项（在 OpenDocDialog 加多选审核）。
5369	- **打印合并表格未移植**（老 `:208`）：**可接受**。该勾选项只在老「整页 window.print」口径下有意义，新窗口单据打印口径下无对应物。
5370	- **「重量合计 0.0」占位未保留**（老 `:263`，`value={"0.0"}` 写死）：**不保留是正确判断**，该占位零信息量，保留反而是保真迷信。
5371	- **批量领料弹窗行为超出老塑胶页**：老塑胶页「按生产单带入」仅为单档 Input.Search（`:277-279`，不带 按货号），多单合并+按货号=true+货号筛选在老系统只属于 `MaterialLineTable.tsx:274-313`（来料领料）。新版把该能力移植到塑胶页，符合需求书「issueBasisPick 场景逐条」的字面要求（spec ✅），但属行为增强，建议与产品确认塑胶侧统一为此口径。代码本身是对 MaterialLineTable 逻辑的忠实照抄（含 `prev.filter(l => l.物料编号)` 空白行替换语义），质量无问题。
5372	- **单文件行数**：`PlasticIssuePage.tsx` 1565 行，处于兄弟页区间（ProductionPage 1551 / PurchaseOrderPage 1726 / PurchaseReceiptPage 1993）之内，**未失控**，但为项目高位；后续可把三个 PickerDialog 抽出，非本次阻断项。
5373	
5374	## 质量结论
5375	
5376	**通过**。Spec 条目全部 ✅，无 Critical/Important；全局约束（dash/emoji/图标/令牌/弹窗四律/骨架零改动/老工程与后端零改动/menuKey 实证）逐项 grep 实证通过；三级链与 issueBasisPick 两条核心契约的测试均为真实断言且与老系统逐字对照一致。遗留为 4 个 Minor 与「批量审核」一个建议跟进的 deferred 缺口。唯一未能独立验证的是测试全绿（按要求未重跑，采信报告自报）。

5551	# Task 7（塑胶入仓单）审查结论
5552	
5553	## Spec 合规（对照需求书逐条）
5554	
5555	- ✅ **入仓新建**：送货单号/供应商/仓库必填，逐级拦截（送货单号 → 供应商 → 仓库 → 有效明细），POST 载荷对照后端 DTO。测试「保存」2 例真实断言（含 POST body `toMatchObject`、空串字段不落、锁定行带生产单号）。
5556	- ✅ **审核（=入仓）/反审核/删除**：单级流转，已审核无删除/审核按钮。后端契约实证 `PlasticReceiptController.cs:63-96`：approve 可返回 `{提示,警告}`、无则 204；web2 侧 `api()` 对 204 返回 undefined（`web2/src/lib/api.ts:27`），`{提示}` 优先展示有专测。
5557	- ✅ **`?单号=` 直开**：**真实测试覆盖**（`plasticReceipt.test.tsx:219-228`）——把首进列表置空，断言 `GET /api/plastic-receipts/SR20260915001` 直发且不依赖最新单。注：老 `PlasticReceiptFormPage` 本身无此参数，`?单号=` 在老系统属塑胶页族能力（`PlasticProcessPurchaseOrderPage.tsx:115` 等），需求书口径为"试点已承诺的塑胶页能力"，新版补齐算履约。
5558	- ✅ **喷油改单自动更新展示**：同步在后端（`SprayPlanReceiptSyncService.cs:107-118` 实证：已审核不动、未审核按映射更新）。前端两点落实：打开弹窗点行强制 `invalidateQueries` 详情、审核/反审核后 `refetch`。测试（`:537-559`）模拟后台 30→55 改单后重开，断言新 GET 发出、55 出现、30 消失，真实有效。
5559	- ✅ **补料入塑胶锁生产单号**：与老系统 `PlasticReceiptFormPage.tsx:123-125` 的 `锁定生产单号: r.生产单号 ? true : undefined` 逐字同口径；老 `LineTable.tsx:51-53` 锁定行 disabled 只读。新版 disabled + `title` tooltip（tooltip 是增强，老系统无，不超口径）；未挂生产单号的行维持可编辑有断言。保存 payload 带锁定行生产单号有断言。
5560	- ✅ **塑胶退仓单入口**：`menu.ts` 占位卡片（置灰），名字与老 `web/src/nav/menuTree.tsx:148` 一致（退仓=plastic-warehouse-returns，与塑胶退料单是两单）。
5561	- ✅ **路由 `/plastic-receipts`**：MainLayout PAGES/PAGE_LOADERS 注册。
5562	
5563	## 全局约束核查
5564	
5565	- ✅ 无 em/en dash、无 emoji：node 扫 8 个改动文件全量，0 命中（报告所称 endpoints.ts "箭头"为 ASCII `->`，无碍）。
5566	- ✅ 图标全部 `@phosphor-icons/react`。
5567	- ✅ 弹窗四律：PickerDialog `bg-[#ffffff]` 不透明 + `max-h-[85vh]`；th `sticky top-0 z-10 bg-white whitespace-nowrap`；编辑/查看网格同规。
5568	- ✅ 骨架组件/hooks 零改动（diff 8 文件不含 components/hooks）；`web/` 与 `src/ErpApi` 零改动。
5569	- ✅ menuKey 实证：`MenuCatalog.cs:48` `("塑胶仓储","塑胶入仓单")` + 控制器 `Menu = "塑胶入仓单"`；DocToolbar 一律显式 `menuKey={MENU}`，测试 perms 行显式锁定该键。
5570	- ⚠️ 视觉令牌：页面用字面 hex（`#1a2330`/`#15803d` 等），均为 `future.css` 令牌同值，且与 Task 5/6 页面同范式（PlasticIssuePage 93 处同款），判合规但不算严格 var() 消费——全工程既有做法，非本任务新增问题。
5571	
5572	## 测试抽查（5+ 真实断言）
5573	
5574	1. `?单号=` 直开：断言 GET detail URL 直发 ✅
5575	2. 带入锁行：`toBeDisabled` + `title` tooltip 文案 + 未锁行 `not.toBeDisabled` ✅
5576	3. 保存 POST body：`toMatchObject` 单头五字段 + 明细 `单价 toBeUndefined`、未锁行 `生产单号 toBeUndefined` ✅
5577	4. 审核后详情 GET 次数 `>=2`（refetch 时机）+ 按钮组切换 ✅
5578	5. 喷油改单 30→55：`queryByText("30")).not.toBeInTheDocument()` 旧值消失 ✅
5579	6. 无单价位：查看态 `***`、编辑网格无单价列、打印 HTML 不含 `<th>金额</th>` ✅
5580	
5581	亲跑验证：`vitest run src/__tests__/plasticReceipt.test.tsx` **27/27 通过**；`tsc -b` **exit 0**（按要求未重跑全套与老系统回归）。
5582	
5583	## 发现
5584	
5585	**Critical：无。**
5586	
5587	**Important（1 项）：**
5588	- 塑胶入仓查询页签未移植，造成**同模块一致性缺口**：Task 5（PurchaseReceiptPage QueryPanel，采购入仓/退仓双页签）与 Task 6（PlasticIssueQueryPanel）都已做"单据+查询"双页签，老系统同路由也有「塑胶入仓查询」（`web/src/App.tsx:274` DocQueryTabs），后端端点与权限键俱在（`MenuCatalog.cs:65` 塑胶报表/塑胶入仓查询、`PlasticReceiptQueryController.cs:15`）。需求书确实未列，不算 spec 违约，但 Task 7 落地后塑胶入仓单成了 web2 里唯一没有查询页签的入仓类单据，用户丧失对账用的明细/汇总查询。**建议立为后续任务**（有现成 QueryPanel 范式，成本低），不宜静默 deferred。报告已在 Concerns 自报。
5589	
5590	**Minor（2 项）：**
5591	- `?单号=` 消费后参数保留在 URL（`PlasticReceiptPage.tsx:786-793`）：dialog 换单后浏览器刷新会跳回参数单；且 keep-alive 下 `autoNoDone` ref 持久，同实例第二次带不同 `?单号=` 进入不会重开。`?ppo=` 已清参数，两者口径不一。当前 web2 内暂无 `?单号=` 跳转方，影响有限。
5592	- `act()` 在审核同时返回 `{提示}+{警告}` 时 toast tone="err"（diff 行 2131），但审核实际已成功，语义偏误导；建议此组合用 ok 或中立 tone。
5593	
5594	**⚠️ 项：**
5595	- 报告的 playwright 新旧对照（截图/JSON 在 `shots/`，脚本未入库）我未逐图核验；老系统 83 文件 424 测试回归与 oxlint 基线未重跑（按审查指示），报告自报均过。
5596	
5597	## 质量结论
5598	
5599	**通过（有条件）**。代码质量良好：纯逻辑正确抽离到 `lib/plasticReceipt.ts` 且单测覆盖，权限位/脱敏/打印 hidePrice 链路完整，API 层 URL 与中文字段照抄老系统并经后端控制器实证，27 个测试断言均为真实行为断言无凑数。Spec 必含场景全 ✅。唯一需要决策的是塑胶入仓查询页签：按 Task 5/6 已建立的双页签惯例，建议补一个后续小任务移植，不作为本任务验收阻塞。

5900	# Task 8（来料领料单）审查结论
5901	
5902	## 一、Spec 合规（逐条对照 task-8-brief.md)
5903	
5904	| 需求 | 结论 | 实证 |
5905	|---|---|---|
5906	| 路由 `/material-issues` | ✅ | `web2/src/layout/MainLayout.tsx:20,33` 注册 PAGES + lazy loader |
5907	| 权限键用最新名「来料领料单」 | ✅ | 后端 `src/ErpApi/Features/Admin/MenuCatalog.cs:17` 物料管理组只有「来料领料单」;`MaterialIssueController.cs:19` Menu="来料领料单"。web2 侧 `MaterialIssuePage.tsx:79` MENU 常量 + DocToolbar 显式 menuKey；全仓 grep 无旧名「领料单」作权限键 |
5908	| 领料新建（依据挑选） | ✅ | 档映射逐字照抄老 `MaterialLineTable.tsx:20-25`（先判半成品因含「成品」子串，说明文案一致）；多单解析/合并/去重/货号筛选直接复用 Task 6 的 `lib/plasticIssue` 纯函数（与 `web/src/__tests__/issueBasisPick.test.ts` 契约同源）；半成品/成品现存档补材料列对应老 `MaterialLineTable.tsx:258` |
5909	| 审核=出库 | ✅ | 后端 `MaterialIssueController.cs:145-160` /approve 是一等端点：门控主管+经理已审、`SyncIssuedWithAuditAsync(单号,true)` 整单置已出扣库存、排期联动，与分次出库共用「已出数量」台账口径。前端三级链文案「待主管审核/待经理审核/待出库/出库完成」逐字=老 `MaterialDocPage.tsx:83-86` |
5910	| 查询双页签 | ✅ | 单据/查询页签 + 明细/汇总子签；URL `/material-issues/issue-query/detail|summary` 原样；筛选（月份/起止/审核情况/物料类别/关键字）、导出/打印、双击回单俱全 |
5911	| 与塑胶领料单口径对齐 | ✅ | 同 FlowSteps 四步、同三级链按钮序、同「审核（出库）」调 approve、同双页签骨架；差异仅在查询权限键，系后端实证差异（塑胶有独立「塑胶领料查询」菜单，来料没有），非实现偏差 |
5912	| 文件清单 | ✅ | 9 文件全在 web2；brief 写 `nav/futureMenu.ts`，实际为 `nav/menu.ts`（文件名漂移，不影响合规）;`nav/menu.ts:50` 装配部组同名叶子保持置灰、`:65` 来料仓组挂路由，与报告一致 |
5913	
5914	## 二、全局约束核查
5915	
5916	- 无 em/en dash、无 emoji:✅（两个新页面 + lib + 测试全 grep 无命中）
5917	- 图标 @phosphor-icons:✅（仅 `@phosphor-icons/react` 一个图标来源）
5918	- 视觉令牌：✅ 页面所有硬编码 hex(#1a2330/#3d4a5c/#5f6b7d/#16a34a/#15803d/#dc2626/#d97706/#b45309）逐一在 `future.css` [data-theme="future"] 令牌中找到；f-panel/f-btn/f-label/f-mono 等类消费未改
5919	- 弹窗四律：✅ `PickerDialog`(`MaterialIssuePage.tsx:598`)bg-[#ffffff] 不透明、max-h-[85vh];LinesEditor/LinesView/查询结果表全部 sticky th + bg-white + z-10 + whitespace-nowrap;max-h 限视口（46vh/52vh)
5920	- 骨架组件/hooks 只消费：✅ diff 文件清单无 components//hooks/ 改动；`MainLayout.tsx` 仅加路由注册
5921	- 老工程 web/ 与后端零改动：✅ diff 9 文件全部 web2
5922	
5923	## 三、重点裁决：分次出库抽屉未移植
5924	
5925	**老系统实证**:`MaterialDocPage.tsx:93-103` 对 material-issues **故意不暴露整单「审核」入口**，出库唯一路径就是 `MaterialIssueOutboundDrawer`(POST /outbound，按行「本次出库」可部分、可跳行，全部出完自动置已审核）。即该抽屉在老 UI 是**主流程**，不是旁路。
5926	
5927	**裁决：Important，不升级 Critical。** 理由：
5928	
5929	1. 需求书 Step 1 明写「审核（=出库）」，且括号注明「与塑胶领料单行为对齐（用户明确要求两边仓口径一致）」——塑胶侧（Task 6）本就无分次出库，本任务实现的就是被点名的口径；
5930	2. 后端 /approve 是一等公民端点，库存台账按「已出数量」实时聚合（`MaterialInventoryService.cs:13`)，与分次出库共用口径，不存在数据口径断裂；
5931	3. 但能力缺口是真实的：老流程支持「先出一部分、剩余再出」，web2 只能整单出。若装配部实际存在分批领料，需老系统兜底或另立任务。报告已在 Concerns 自报，未隐瞒。
5932	
5933	**建议**：作为后续任务单独立项（或在迁移切换前由业务确认「来料仓不需要分次出库」)，不阻塞本任务验收。
5934	
5935	## 四、测试抽查（26 条，grep 计数=26，与报告一致）
5936	
5937	抽查 7 条均真实断言、无套套逻辑：
5938	
5939	- 三级链（materialIssue.test.tsx):assert 精确端点 URL `supervisor-approve`→`manager-approve`→`approve`、状态文案逐级迁移、审核后删除入口消失。库存扣减属后端职责，前端测到「端点+refetch 后状态」为止，粒度合理
5940	- 多单调入：assert 2 次 issue-basis 请求、URL 含 `档=来料`+`按货号=true`、合并后 HH-A 在 HH-B 前、默认全选、货号筛选 options=["","HH-A","HH-B"]
5941	- 半成品仓档：assert `档=半成品`、**不**带 `按货号`、材料列补「半成品」
5942	- 保存成功：assert POST 载荷字段（部门默认装配部/仓库来料仓/明细数量转数值/备注去空白）
5943	- 权限位 4 条：保存/审核/删除/反审核/打印/打开位各自门控，含「无打开位查询页签整页无权」
5944	- 查询过滤：assert `审核情况=`/`物料类别=`/`keyword=` 下发 + 双击行 GET 整单回单据页签
5945	- 打印：有/无「单价」位两例，价格列出现与消失都断言
5946	
5947	## 五、发现清单
5948	
5949	**Critical**：无。
5950	
5951	**Important**:
5952	1. 分次出库抽屉（POST /outbound 部分出库）未移植 — 老 UI 主流程出库路径，见上裁决。出处：`web/src/pages/materials/MaterialIssueOutboundDrawer.tsx:9`、`web/src/pages/materials/MaterialDocPage.tsx:103`；新侧对应位置 `web2/src/pages/MaterialIssuePage.tsx:1046`（仅整单 approve)。
5953	
5954	**Minor**:
5955	1. 删除按钮对「未审核但有已出数量」的单仍会渲染（后端 `MaterialIssueService.cs:128` 会 Conflict 拦截并回消息），前端只靠 toast 兜底。老系统同样如此（口径未变），不算回归，仅记录。`web2/src/pages/MaterialIssuePage.tsx` 删除 action 段。
5956	2. 下推 `?basis=` 的档取自初始表单闭包（默认来料仓）并带 `eslint-disable exhaustive-deps`(`MaterialIssuePage.tsx:891` 附近）；行为与报告自述一致（档随默认来料仓），可用但脆弱，仓库默认策略变更时会静默跟随。
5957	
5958	**⚠️ 项**:
5959	- 报告自报的另两项缺口（半成品/成品「入仓单带入」、批量审核/复制单）经抽查属实：老 `MaterialLineTable.tsx:103-157` 确有入仓单带入，新侧未做；均在必含场景之外，与 Task 5/6/7 同范式，记录即可。
5960	- 查询页签与单据共用权限键与塑胶侧分离模式不同，属后端实证差异，报告与代码注释都已标注，无误导。
5961	- 报告声称的 playwright 截图/新旧对照数据不在审查包内，本轮未复核该证据本身（对照 JSON 字段级 match 仅采信报告自述）。
5962	
5963	## 六、质量结论
5964	
5965	**通过（附 1 项 Important 跟踪项）**。实现保真度高：档映射、状态文案、说明文案逐字对照老代码；依据挑选纯函数与塑胶侧同源复用，两仓口径天然一致；权限键实证扎实；测试是真断言而非摆设。分次出库缺口由 spec 的「审核=出库+两边仓一致」豁免，但因它是老系统主流程出库路径，必须在迁移收口前由业务确认或另立任务，建议写进批次级 TODO。
5966	
5967	</details>

6188	## 发现
6189	
6190	### Important
6191	
6192	1. **下加工单跳塑胶加工采购单：静默断链且成功提示不可见**（`web2/src/pages/AssemblyPurchasePage.tsx:744`）。`navigate("/plastic-process-purchase-orders?单号=...")` 落入未注册路由，`MainLayout.tsx:138` 的 `showHome = !PAGES[path]` 使其渲染宫格首页；更糟的是成功 toast（含新单号 SJ-NEW）设在被 keep-alive `hidden` 的本页上（`MainLayout.tsx:171`），用户点"确定"后看到的就是首页，**没有任何确认、单号也拿不到**——比"跳错页"更差，是"操作结果对用户的彻底丢失"。POST 本身成功、数据无损，故不升 Critical。
6193	   **裁决建议**：不接受"等后续批次页面落地即通"的现状，本批就应修。推荐最小修法：跳转前判断目标路由是否已注册，未注册则不跳，留在本页并把 toast 改为"塑胶加工采购单 SJ-NEW 已创建（该单据页后续批次上线，请记下单号）"。不推荐跳老系统深链：现有"回旧版"先例（`TopBar.tsx:128`）是硬编码 `localhost:5173` 的顶层链接，深链会把 web2 页面逻辑耦合到 dev 地址，比留页提示更重。
6194	
6195	### Minor
6196	
6197	2. "行级空→单头"后端回落路径无直接断言：`collectProductionLines` 测试覆盖了同下标优先与按货号匹配，但没有"完全无匹配产品行 → 客户字段 undefined 交由后端回落单头"的用例。实现正确（照抄旧页口径），仅测试缺口。
6198	3. 需求书文件名 `web2/src/nav/futureMenu.ts` 与实际 `web2/src/nav/menu.ts` 不符（nav/ 下只有 menu.ts），属需求书过时，实现改对了地方。
6199	4. 页面拆成 3 文件（Page/Header/Tables）超出需求书 Files 清单，但 1548 行单文件拆分有先例且合理，不算违规。
6200	
6201	### ⚠️ 项（不阻塞）
6202	
6203	- 生产制单选择器 onProductionPick 无单测（报告自报，交互模式同 Task 6/7 已验证，可接受）。
6204	- datalist 替代 antd 搜索下拉，无高亮匹配项，交互略简（报告自报，jsdom 可测性换来的合理取舍）。
6205	- usePerms 试点 admin 端点为 Task 2 遗留，非本任务范围。
6206	- 旧测试还断言了 `navigate` 的 URL 参数本身，新测试塑胶路径无法断言落点（页面不存在），与 Important 1 同根。
6207	
6208	## 质量结论
6209	
6210	实现质量高：纯逻辑抽到 `lib/assemblyPurchase.ts` 页面与测试共用，行为逐条照抄旧页并带注释标注出处；测试为真实断言非渲染冒烟；约束项全部 grep 实证通过；menuKey 实证链路完整。**有条件通过**：Important 1（跳转断链+toast 不可见）建议在本批修掉（一行路由存在性判断+toast 文案），Minor 2 的回落测试用例可顺手补上；其余 ⚠️ 项均有报告自报与先例支撑，不阻塞合入。

6283	## 发现 1(Important)：下加工单跳塑胶加工采购单断链+toast 不可见 — **ADDRESSED**
6284	
6285	- **路由注册判断**:`AssemblyPurchasePage.tsx:744-756`，塑胶分支先取 `target = "/plastic-process-purchase-orders"`,`MENU_PATHS.has(target)` 为 false 时不 `navigate`，留在本页并 `setToast({ text: 塑胶加工采购单 ${created} 已创建(该单据页后续批次上线,请记下单号) })`；装配分支（`:758-760`）逻辑未动，仍跳 `/assembly-purchases?单号=` 直开。修法与裁决要求逐条吻合。
6286	- **MENU_PATHS 真实性**:`web2/src/nav/menu.ts:118-120`,`MENU_PATHS` 不是硬编码清单，而是从 `MENU_TREE` 有 path 的叶子动态 flatMap 得出。与 `MainLayout.tsx:22-33` 的 `PAGES` 对照：MENU_TREE 唯一路径共 10 条（`/production`、`/plastic-issues`、`/assembly-purchases`、`/purchase-orders`、`/purchase-receipts`、`/material-issues`、`/purchase-returns`、`/material-inventory`、`/plastic-receipts`、`/accounts`),PAGES 键同样 10 条，**集合完全相等，当前无漂移**;`/plastic-process-purchase-orders` 确实不在其中，判断结果正确。残余隐患（非本次破坏）:MENU_PATHS 与 PAGES 是两个并列数据源，靠注释（menu.ts:115）约定同步维护，未来加页面时漏改任一侧仍会漂移——建议后续批次考虑从 PAGES 单侧导出，本轮可放行。
6287	- **toast 可见性**:`AssemblyPurchasePage.tsx:1555` 在本页内渲染 `<DocToast>`，不跳走即页面不被 keep-alive 隐藏，toast 真实可见；文案含新单号 `created`，符合要求。
6288	- **测试真实性**:`assemblyPurchase.test.tsx:397-403`,POST 断言在前（387-396),toast 断言用全文案正则 `/塑胶加工采购单 SJ-NEW 已创建\(该单据页后续批次上线,请记下单号\)/`——旧代码 toast 无后缀、必然失配，**这是对修复的真实回归断言**(SJ-NEW 来自 fetch 桩 149-150 行）。一点诚实保留：`renderWithProviders`(test/setup.tsx:7-16）用裸 `MemoryRouter` 无 `<Routes>`，即使发生 navigate 页面也不会卸载，所以 `:403` 的「装配加工采购单（新建）」对「不跳走」是弱断言（在 harness 下恒真）；真正的鉴别力在 toast 文案差异。属于 Minor 级测试强度问题，不影响修复正确性。

6838	## 逐项裁决
6839	
6840	**1. 数据源切换 + 响应形状适配 — ADDRESSED**
6841	- 后端实证：`AuthController.cs:26-31` `GET /auth/me/permissions` 返回 `perm.GetByUserAsync(name)`，即 `PermissionService.cs:7-27` 直读 `userbqrpower` 组装的 `IReadOnlyDictionary<string, PermissionFlags>`；`PermissionFlags.cs:7-15` 的 9 个属性就是中文位名（打开/保存/删除/打印/单价/金额/审核/反审核/功能）。JSON 形状 = `{菜单: {打开..功能}}`，与 web2 新增 `MyPermMap`（types.ts）完全吻合，也与旧系统 `web/src/auth/permissions.ts:5` 的 `PermMap` 同构；旧系统 `PermissionContext.tsx:11` 用的正是此端点。
6842	- 适配层确实只在 hook 内：`usePerms.ts` queryFn 换为 `authApi.myPermissions()`，`can()` 由数组 find 改为 `!!map[menuKey]?.[bit]`。`userPermApi` 仍保留在 `endpoints.ts:532` 供 AccountsPage 使用，符合"admin 端点只供账号管理页"的注释口径。
6843	
6844	**2. 放行/严格/缺失拒绝语义 — ADDRESSED**
6845	`usePerms.ts:23-27`：`!bit → true`（不变）；`!map → true`（未登录/加载中/失败的放行分支不变，queryFn 失败时 `q.data` 为 undefined）；就位后 `map[menuKey]?.[bit]` 缺失菜单 → undefined → false（拒绝），与原 `find` 后 `!row → false` 等价。
6846	
6847	**3. 9 个测试桩切换、断言不变 — ADDRESSED**
6848	diff 触及的测试文件正好 9 个（assemblyPurchase、docSkeleton、materialIssue、plasticIssue、plasticReceipt、production、purchaseOrder、purchaseReceipt、scheduling）。每个文件的 hunk 只改两行：import 加 `permRowsToMap`，桩路由 `endsWith("/perms")` → `endsWith("/me/permissions")`（docSkeleton 改判 `/api/auth/me/permissions`）。perms 字面量与全部行为断言零改动。`setup.tsx` 的 `permRowsToMap` 丢弃 `组`、以 `菜单` 为 key，正确映射新形状。
6849	
6850	**4. 两处 Minor — ADDRESSED**
6851	- `ScheduleProductionDialog.tsx` toast tone `err → ok`（diff 确认，仅提示性文案）。
6852	- `ScheduleImportDialog.tsx:295` `#92610a` → `text-[var(--warning,#d97706)]`；令牌定义于 `web2/src/future.css:40`，且同写法已在 PurchaseOrderPage/AssemblyPurchaseHeader/ProductionPage 多处使用，风格一致。
6853	
6854	**页面零改动核实**：diff 文件清单中除两个对话框外无任何页面文件；grep 确认 13 处 `const { can } = usePerms()` 调用点均未在 diff 中。

6858	未发现 Critical/Important 级新破坏。一个无足轻重的观察：queryKey `["user-perms", user]` 未随数据源改名，但 TanStack Query 缓存纯内存无持久化，旧形状缓存不会跨会话残留，不构成问题。
6859	
6860	## 报告事实错误（一处，重要）
6861	
6862	`perm-source-report.md:29` 声称：**"旧系统 web/ 没有账号权限管理页（grep -rln "admin/accounts" web/src/pages/ 无命中），从不调用该 PUT……该隐患是 web2 新增页独有的"** —— 此说法不属实。
6863	
6864	- `web/src/pages/admin/AccountPage.tsx` 存在且就是账号权限管理页：其 `PermDrawer`（第 144-179 行）调用 `userPermApi.get` / `userPermApi.save`（第 154、175 行）。
6865	- grep 无命中是因为 URL 字面量封装在 `web/src/api/admin.ts:47-52`（`/admin/accounts/{user}/perms` 的 GET/PUT），不在 pages/ 目录——报告的 grep 范围选错了。
6866	- 推论修正：PUT 整组替换会清掉非 MenuCatalog 行（如「生产排期」）的隐患**旧系统同样存在**，并非 web2 独有；报告基于错误前提得出的"不存在可对齐的旧行为"也不成立（旧行为就是同样会清行）。不过最终结论"纯前端无法加保护、需后端改动、超出本次约束"依然成立，不受影响。
6867

6869	
6870	**通过（PASS）**。修复本身四项裁决要求全部 ADDRESSED，无新破坏。报告的测试/验证结论可信，但其 accounts 章节的前提事实错误（旧系统有 AccountPage.tsx 且同样调用整组替换 PUT）需更正：该隐患是新旧系统共有的后端语义问题，建议在遗留记录中改写，避免后续裁决基于"web2 独有"的错误定性。

7052	
7053	## Spec 合规（对照 task-11-brief.md 逐条）
7054

7056	| --- | --- |

7070	## 发现
7071	
7072	**Critical：无。**
7073	
7074	**Important 1 — 经理反审核批准动作在 web2 无处可用，报告理由不实。** web2 全工程 grep 无 `approveUnapproveRequest`/`rejectUnapproveRequest`,`productionApi` 只有 `requestUnapprove`(endpoints.ts:112);ProductionPage 仅有「申请反审核」和「反审核申请中…待经理批准」徽标（ProductionPage.tsx:1274)，没有任何同意/拒绝入口。报告声称「审批动作走 /production 单据页本身」不成立——跳单据页后经理无法完成审批。按 brief 清单该抽屉不在交付范围内，**defer 本身可接受**，但必须登记为后续批次的明确缺口（否则审批流在 web2 断链）。连带：`ProductionPage.tsx:1476` 注释「提交后经理在消息中心批准/拒绝」对 web2 已过时，应改。
7075	
7076	**Minor 1 — 分页 select 的 Tailwind 类是死类。** `.f-input` 无层叠且带 `height:44px`(future.css:153-155，注释自述优先级高于 Tailwind 工具类），因此 `MessagesPage.tsx:875` 的 `h-9 px-2 text-sm` 均不生效，select 实际 44px 高于旁边 h-9(36px）按钮。width 修复本身正确（w-24 定宽容器包裹，不动 future.css，登录页等既有输入框零影响 ✅)，仅高度不齐，视觉小瑕疵。
7077	
7078	**Minor 2 — 老工程对照面。** `web/src/__tests__/messages.test.ts` 仅 1 个 API 形状用例；新 8 个用例在覆盖面（分页/筛选/分流/裁决/铃铛）上远超老工程，属增强而非对齐缺口。
7079	
7080	## ⚠️ 项
7081	
7082	- **MaterialIssuePage `?doc=` 深链越 brief 清单**:13 行，渲染期派生 + effect 清参，与 ProductionPage `?mo=`(ProductionPage.tsx:830-840）逐行同构；brief 接口要求「跳对应单据页」，无此深链则领料审批消息无处可跳，判定**合理必需**，报告已自报，接受。
7083	- **`#9aa5b1` 不在 future.css 令牌中**，但系全工程 24 文件（含 LoginPage/MainLayout）的既有惯例灰，非本任务引入，不算 Task 11 违规，建议批次收尾时统一收进令牌。
7084	- **测试抽查（5/8)**：用例 1(query 参数+同源未读数）、3（翻页 page=2 + 切 50 回 page=1)、4（精确 POST URL + 深链 lastLoc + 未读数重拉 >1 次）、5（负断言不发 POST + 深链）、8（铃铛跳 /messages）均为真实断言，无摆设测试。
7085	- MainLayout diff 上下文可见 `/purchase-returns` loader 复用 PurchaseReceiptPage — 非本任务引入，仅提示后续核对。
7086	
7087	## 质量结论
7088	
7089	**通过，可合并。** 行为照抄老系统忠实（含 `slice(0,19).replace("T"," ")`、ID 归一化、类型分流口径），裁决模式与 Task 9/10 惯例一致，测试真实且实测 8/8 绿。唯一实质缺口是经理反审核批准动作整体未落地（Important 1)，属范围外 defer，但必须挂账并修正 ProductionPage.tsx:1476 的过时注释，否则 web2 审批流闭环断裂这一点会被遗忘。

7177	## 逐项裁决
7178	
7179	### 1. （裁决补做）经理反审核批准/拒绝动作 — **ADDRESSED**（带两点保留）
7180	
7181	- **端点逐字一致**：`web2/src/api/endpoints.ts:118-121` 新增 `POST /production/{单号}/unapprove-request/approve|reject`，与老系统 `web/src/api/production.ts:92-95` 逐字相同；`endpoints.ts:450-453` 新增 `POST /styles/{款号}/bom-reverse-audit-request/approve|reject`，与老系统 `web/src/api/styles.ts:166-169` 逐字相同。两侧均用 `enc()` 编码。
7182	- **载荷一致（无 body)**：老系统 `api.post(url)` 无 data；新系统 `api<void>(url, { method: "POST" })` 无 body，且 API 签名只收单号/款号，无从传载荷。
7183	- **抽屉行为对齐**：老系统 `web/src/pages/MessagesPage.tsx:53-70`（查看先标记已读再开抽屉）、`:73-89`（BOM 走 stylesApi、成功后关抽屉 + load() + refreshBell())；新系统 `web2/src/pages/MessagesPage.tsx:115-129`（标记已读后开弹窗）、`:150-176`(decide 同样 BOM 分流，成功后关弹窗 + `refreshAll()` .invalidate messages-page/messages-unread-count/messages-panel 三键，`:102-106`，与老系统 load+refreshBell 等效）。
7184	- **权限门槛**：老系统前端**完全不校验**(`web/src/pages/MessagesPage.tsx:206-210`，按钮只看消息类型），后端 `IsManagerAsync`(`ProductionService.cs:505`、`StyleService.cs:428`，口径 = `职称='经理'` 或 admin）兜底。修复加了前端门 `admin || can("生产制单","审核")`(`MessagesPage.tsx:71-73`)——这与老系统"无门"不同，但审查发现本身要求"无权限不渲染"测试，加门是裁决的必然结果；且与 web2 既有惯例一致（三级流转经理审核按钮均挂 `perm:"审核"`，如 `MaterialIssuePage.tsx:1044` 经 `DocToolbar.tsx:75` 过滤）。**保留①**：门槛口径是权限位而非后端的 `职称=经理`，理论上"有经理职称但无生产制单·审核位"的用户在 web2 看不到按钮（老系统能看到），实际风险低（消息接收人已被后端过滤为经理+admin)；另 BOM 类消息也用"生产制单"位而非工程BOM菜单位，属近似。
7185	- **测试真实断言**:4 条新用例（`web2/src/__tests__/messages.test.tsx` "反审核审批弹窗" describe）逐字断言 URL+method（如 `/api/production/SC20260916001/unapprove-request/approve`、`/api/styles/92125A-S001/bom-reverse-audit-request/approve`)、成功文案、关窗重拉、BOM 无"查看单据"、无权限不渲染按钮，均非空断言。
7186	- **保留②(nit)**：成功文案用半角逗号"已同意反审核，已回到未审核"，老系统是全角"已同意反审核，已回到未审核"(`web/src/pages/MessagesPage.tsx:82`),"照抄"说法在标点上有出入；弹窗说明文案同样半角化。纯装饰性。
7187	
7188	### 2. (Minor) ProductionPage.tsx:1476 注释 — **ADDRESSED**
7189	
7190	注释原文"提交后经理在消息中心批准/拒绝"(`web2/src/pages/ProductionPage.tsx:1476`）在 round 1 前是过时承诺（消息中心无审批动作）;round 1 把该动作实现后，注释与实际已一致，未改注释本身属合理处理。
7191	
7192	### 3. (Minor) 分页 select 高度 — **ADDRESSED**（带保留）
7193	
7194	- 修法正确：`future.css:184-188` 新增无层叠修饰类 `.f-input-slim`（与 `.f-input-icon` 同模式），只用于 `MessagesPage.tsx:360`;grep 确认全仓库无其他使用处，`.f-input` 本体未动，其他 f-input 使用处零影响。
7195	- **保留**:web2 是 Tailwind v4(package.json:29，工具类在 @layer)，无层叠 `.f-btn { height:44px }`(`future.css:116`）同样压住按钮上的 `h-9`，即分页按钮实际渲染 44px 而非 36px。修复后 select=36px、按钮=44px，靠 `items-center` 垂直居中——高度并不相等（修复注释"与 h-9(36px）按钮对齐"的前提不成立）；而修复前两者同为 44px 本就等高。作者称已 playwright 截图复核视觉整齐。属 Minor 观感取舍，非破坏，是否接受留给主裁决。
7196

7198	
7199	无。diff 只触 4 个文件，均为新增/局部修改：测试桩扩充不影响其他用例；`view()` 对审批类消息改开弹窗是裁决要求本身；老系统（web/）与后端零改动。`DocToast`/`Dialog`/`usePerms`/`permRowsToMap` 均为既有基建复用。
7200	
7201	## 总结论
7202	
7203	**三项全部 ADDRESSED，可过。** 端点/载荷与老系统逐字一致有实证，测试断言真实，select 修法不影响其他 f-input。两个非阻塞保留供知悉：①前端权限门用"生产制单·审核"位而非后端 `职称=经理` 口径（审查要求所致，后端兜底）;②成功文案标点为半角、"h-9=36px"前提与实际 44px 渲染不符——均为 Minor 级，不建议因此打回。按要求未重跑测试套件，验证基于代码与端点双向比对。

7421	## useTableDensity 与密度生效机制
7422	
7423	- 签名、三档行高、持久化均合规。`virtualizer.measure()` 修复真实：TanStack Virtual 的 measurementsCache 不随 estimateSize 变化失效，`measure()` 清缓存按新 estimateSize 重测，机制正确；`useEffect(..., [density, virtualizer])` 是同步外部系统的合规写法，非 setState-in-effect。
7424	- ⚠️2：jsdom 把 `@tanstack/react-virtual` 整体桩掉（measure 为 no-op），**单测并未覆盖 measure() 修复本身**，行高断言走的是桩里 estimateSize 全量渲染路径。修复有效性仅有报告自报的 playwright 截图佐证（脚本在 `/tmp/pwshot` 未入库，我无法复核）。机制原理可信，判 Minor 测试盲区，不阻塞。
7425	
7426	## 测试抽查（实际跑了全部 9 例）
7427	
7428	`npx vitest run src/__tests__/inventory.test.tsx` → **9/9 通过**（1.46s）。重点看了 4 例：渲染+货号+负库存红卡、查询参数/重置、导出打印桩断言+空结果不导出、密度三档+持久化+重开保持。桩法合理（downloadCsv/printTable 桩掉保真身 buildCsv；localStorage 走真身）。
7429	
7430	## 约束 grep
7431	
7432	- em/en dash：`web2/src` 全目录 grep `—/–` 无匹配 ✅
7433	- emoji：变更文件 emoji 区段无匹配 ✅
7434	- 图标：Export/Printer 均 from `@phosphor-icons/react` ✅
7435	- 视觉令牌：新增代码用 `f-btn/f-label/f-panel`；密度切换的 `#16a34a/12` 等 hex 为既有代码非本任务新增（diff 上下文证实）✅
7436	- portal 弹层 / 弹窗四律：本任务无新增弹层弹窗，N/A ✅
7437	
7438	## 发现
7439	
7440	- **Critical**：无
7441	- **Important**：无
7442	- **Minor**：
7443	  1. `InventoryPage.tsx:170-180` 仓库下拉数据源是 `list({})`（默认含零=false）去重，零库存仓库不进选项；且 `EXPORT_COLS` 与 `columns` 两处维护，列漂移风险（测试已锁定 8 列标题，缓解）
7444	  2. `virtualizer.measure()` 修复无单测覆盖（见 ⚠️2）
7445	- **⚠️ 项**：
7446	  1. **仓库下拉 vs 旧页自由文本**：后端 `仓库` 是精确匹配（`=@wh`），旧页自由文本同样精确匹配，功能等价；唯一差异是"有仓库档案但当前无库存"的仓库选不到（旧页可手输+含零库存查该仓全部物料为 0 的行）。影响边缘，报告已如实披露并建议改仓库档案字典源。**判可 deferred**，不阻塞。
7447	  2. **密度只落地库存页 vs brief"全站表格生效"**：brief Interfaces 行描述的是 hook 契约（共用 key 即全站机制），机制已就位；web2 无共享表组件，其余页接入需先抽组件，属后续立项范畴，报告披露清晰。**判可 deferred**。
7448	
7449	## 质量结论
7450	
7451	**通过**。改动最小且精准（删私有密度、接 hook、补货号列、补导出打印、修真实虚拟滚动 bug），注释与测试契约都对齐老系统，9 例单测实跑全过，全局约束零违规。两个 ⚠️ 均为已披露的口径/范围问题，建议按报告 Concerns 立项跟进（共享表组件内置 measure() + 仓库字典数据源），无需本任务返工。

7670	35	    Your review is read-only on this checkout. Do not mutate the working tree, the index, HEAD, or branch state in any way. Use tools like `git show`, `git diff`, and `git log` to inspect history. If you need a working copy of a different revision, check it out into a separate temporary directory (e.g. `git worktree add /tmp/review-[SHA] [SHA]`) — never move HEAD on this checkout.

RETURN : RECEIPT`）+ `MainLayout.tsx` 中 `/purchase-receipts` 与 `/purchase-returns` 映射同一组件且所有已开标签常驻挂载。`hidden` 只是视觉隐藏，两个实例都订阅 location：打开"采购退仓单"标签的瞬间，已开的入仓实例 pathname 变为 `/purchase-returns`，`DocPage key={kind.key}` 变 key 整体重挂载——**入仓标签里未保存的表单立即被销毁**，且切回时又是一次全新挂载。反向同理。这正好击穿 keep-alive 标签页的核心承诺。逐任务审和 vitest（单页直渲）都看不到这个交互。
7867	   修法（小）：拆两个固定 kind 的薄包装组件（`PurchaseReceiptsPage`/`PurchaseReturnsPage` 各渲染 `<DocPage kind={RECEIPT|RETURN} />`），PAGE_LOADERS 分别指向；补一个 MainLayout 级测试：开两个标签、入仓侧改表单、切到退仓再切回，断言表单仍在。
7868	
7869	**Important**
7870	
7871	2. **keep-alive 下跨页数据永不刷新** — `InventoryPage.tsx:189-193`（`["material-inventory", applied]`）与 `HomePage.tsx` 两张卡只在挂载时拉取；keep-alive 下切回不触发 refetchOnMount，全局 `refetchOnWindowFocus:false`，各单据页审核/保存成功只 invalidate 自己的命名空间（如 `PlasticReceiptPage.tsx:843-869` 只刷 `plastic-receipt/*`）。结果：在采购入仓审核一单后切到库存页/首页，看到的是一个会话期内任意陈旧的库存数；甚至库存页点"查询"而筛选条件不变时（queryKey 哈希相同）也不刷新。老系统每次进页重拉，这是新版引入的回归。修法任选：各单据变更成功后补 `qc.invalidateQueries({queryKey:["material-inventory"]})` + `["home-*"]`；或库存页查询按钮在条件不变时显式 `listQuery.refetch()`。建议至少做前者再发。

9495	5. **工序/物料页签 ✅** — 按选中货号过滤、null 单价 `***`、需订>0 红、价格列按「单价+金额」位整列移除、快照列口径，与老 552-578、683-715 一致。
9496	6. **新建态 BOM 实时预览 ✅** — 触发条件、货号在 bom-headers 用货号否则回落 BOM款号、空编号过滤、「BOM 预览（N 项 · 保存后按数量展开）」标题，与老 556-578 逐行对应。
9497	
9498	## 发现
9499	
9500	**Important（1 条）**
9501	- `web2/src/pages/ProductionStartupDialog.tsx:110` — `<thead className="sticky top-0 bg-white">`：sticky 加在 `thead` 上，违反首批计划弹窗四律明文"sticky 表头加在 **th** 上（不是 thead/tr）且带不透明底 + z-10"。全工程其余 20+ 处都在 th 上，且有契约测试守护此约定（`docSkeleton.test.tsx:141`）。Chrome 下 thead sticky 不生效，58 行缺口列表在 46vh 容器内滚动时表头会丢失；也缺 `z-10`。修复是把 sticky/z-10/不透明底下沉到每个 th。
9502	
9503	**Minor（3 条）**
9504	- `productionAddons.test.tsx:356-378` — 一键启动测试只断言四个统计标签与「缺 40」「够」标签，未断言统计数值本身（2/150/1/40）；数值正确性目前仅靠 playwright 截图。
9505	- `web2/src/components/doc/ImageNotesPanel.tsx:1072-1085` — 大图预览 Dialog 无 `DialogDescription`，radix 会在控制台报 a11y 警告（ConfirmDialog 的 description 可选模式可参照；弹窗四律原文未强制，故仅 Minor）。
9506	- `ProductionPage.tsx` MO 水合用渲染期 setState（与页内既有 `hydrated` 模式一致）：TanStack structural sharing 下相同数据 refetch 不会覆盖在录内容，但若跨页失效触发 refetch 且服务端数据已变，会覆盖未保存编辑；`refetchOnWindowFocus:false` 已盖住主路径，属可接受的口径放宽（老系统只在 loadDoc 载入）。
9507

10007	审查完成，所有证据已核实。以下是审查结论。
10008	
10009	## Batch 0F 审查结论
10010	
10011	### Spec 合规：✅ 通过
10012	
10013	- 验收基准达成：`含零库存=true` 返回主档全量（有库存显实数、无库存显 0）；`=false` 行为不变（实证 140 行，零库存 0 行）。修复后 true=157 = 140 台账 + 17 主档零库存，算术自洽。
10014	- 改动范围合规：仅后端服务 + 测试 + 报告 3 个文件，`git diff --stat 137ae29..a7d318a` 实证 web/web2 零改动；工作树干净。
10015	- 基线合规：892=891+1，9 个既有失败不新增（与用户给定基线一致；未重跑测试，按指示采信报告）。
10016	- 无 dash/emoji：代码与测试注释干净；报告中"——"为中文破折号，与计划文档自身文风一致，不属 UI 文案违规。
10017	
10018	### 重点核查结果
10019	
10020	**FULL OUTER JOIN 修法正确。** 粒度分析：`账` CTE 按（物料编号×仓库）聚合，`主档` CTE 按物料编号预聚合（MAX 收敛重复主档行），JOIN 键唯一侧在主档——主档 1 行对台账 N 行（每仓库一行）产生 N 行，无扇出重复；主档独有 1 行显 0；台账独有按仓库出行。与旧写法（GROUP BY + MAX）语义等价且消除了丢行。
10021	
10022	**false 分支确实未动。** `MaterialInventoryService.cs:116-130` 在 diff 中仅为上下文行，逐字未变；测试也断言了 false 行为（`MaterialInventoryDbTests.cs:268-270`）。
10023	
10024	**游离行 NULL 处理正确。** `MaterialStockRow` 的 货号/物料类别/每单位数值/仓库位置 本就是 `string?`（MaterialStockRow.cs:10-13），false 分支 LEFT JOIN 对同样的游离行也回 NULL——API 形状无新增变化，前端已在 false 模式下承受该形状。
10025	
10026	**根因可信。** "140 行台账全部游离"这一强断言有内证：若 140 行与 17 行主档有交集，FULL OUTER JOIN 结果必小于 157，实证恰为 157 且零库存行恰为 17，交集为空被算术证明。FK_203_查找 真实存在于 `db/02_rebuild_relations.sql:243`；not-trusted 属历史导入形态，合理。
10027	
10028	**测试真实断言且有判别力。** 旧 SQL 下 P3MZB 不在主档，`Assert.Single(withZero, x => x.物料编号 == "P3MZB")`（MaterialInventoryDbTests.cs:264）必红——该测试确实能守住修复。NOCHECK 复现游离行 + finally 中先 CleanupZeroStock 再 `WITH CHECK CHECK` 的顺序正确（先删游离行再校验，否则恢复会失败）；断言失败也走 finally，约束可恢复。
10029	
10030	**类别筛选游离行不可见：可接受。** false 分支 LEFT JOIN 主档后按 `m.物料类别` 过滤，游离行同样被排除——true 分支与之口径一致，且报告已自报该行为。这是既有口径的延续，非新引入缺陷。
10031	
10032	### 发现
10033	
10034	- Critical：无。
10035	- Important：无。
10036	- Minor：
10037	  1. `src/ErpApi/Engines/Inventory/IMaterialInventoryService.cs:8` 接口注释仍是旧口径"=true：以物料资料为主档，每个分类显示全部物料，无库存显示 0"，未提及现在包含台账游离行。报告称"注释同步更新"，但实际只更新了实现注释（MaterialInventoryService.cs:78-79），契约层注释不完整。建议补一句"含不在主档的历史台账行"。
10038	  2. `MaterialInventoryService.cs:107`：FULL OUTER JOIN 的 WHERE 引用 `m.物料类别`，@cat 非空时退化为排除游离行——这是有意为之且与 false 一致，但 SQL 层面值得在注释里点明（现注释只说了丢行根因，未说 @cat 对游离行的语义）。
10039	
10040	### ⚠️ 项
10041	
10042	- 报告未记录"先红后绿"的红测环节（只报了最终 892 绿）。测试判别力可由代码检查推出（旧 SQL 下必红），但过程证据缺失；后续批次建议报告注明红测截图/输出。
10043	- 测试在共享 erp_test 上临时 NOCHECK 约束：正常路径 finally 可恢复，但进程被杀（kill -9）会留下 NOCHECK 状态；xUnit 跨测试类并行时窗口期内其他类插入明细行不受校验。低概率，可接受。

10045	
10046	### 质量结论
10047	
10048	修复方案正确且克制：只动 true 分支、false 零接触、粒度分析无重复行风险、测试有真实判别力且现场可恢复、实证数字内证根因。两处 Minor 均为注释完整性问题，不阻塞验收。建议合入，接口注释可在后续批次顺手补齐。
